10 月金融数据点评：如何看社融增速回升？

[Table_Author] 
 沈夏宜 分析师  魏争 分析师

Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com Email:weizheng@lczq.com  
证书:S1320523020004 证书:S1320524100001

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
增 6983 亿元，是当月社融的主要支撑。一是去年同期到期规模较高，形
成较低基数。今年 3 月以来，月均国债发行稳定在 1.3 万亿左右，国债净
融资规模与当月到期规模关系较大；二是专项债发行仍保持一定强度，当
月新增专项债 3280 亿元，同比多增 406 亿元。 
企业债券、委托贷款、外币贷款均同比多增。当月新增企业债券 3446 亿
元，同比多增 946 亿元。当月债券利率下行，推动更多企业“以债替贷”
的融资转变；当月新增委托贷款 1862 亿元，同比多增 208 亿元。新型政
策性金融工具在 10 月全部投放完毕，集中投放对委托贷款形成支撑；当
月新增外币贷款 95 亿，同比多增 295 亿。10 月国内进口同比增长 
41.6%，进口付汇需求较强。此外，人民币汇率偏强亦对外币贷款需求形
成一定支撑。 
M1 增速回升。当月 M1 同比增长 4.4%，较上月回升 1.2 个百分点。一是
去年同期基数偏低；二是新型政策性金融工具集中投放，以及 PSL 利率
下调、支持扩围，对企业活期资金形成一定支撑；三是当月人民币延续升
值趋势，持续推动企业结汇意愿。 
M2 增速小幅回落。10 月 M2 同比增长 7.1%，较上月回落 0.1 个百分
点。一是当月社融主要增量来自于政府部门，但当月财政存款同比多增
6800 亿，向企业和居民部门释放偏慢；二是信贷增速续降，存款派生效
应偏弱。 
社融增速有望保持平稳。往前看，一是货币端，10 月末央行优化公开市
场操作机制，将隔夜逆回购转为日常操作，强化短端利率调控，有助于稳
定银行体系流动性；二是需求端，10 月 19 日，发改委等部门出台消费基
础设施建设指导意见，支持商业、文旅、交通、养老等领域设施建设，有
望拓宽相关领域融资需求；三是财政端，地方政府债务结存限额发行使用
进入年末收官阶段，叠加前期政策资金逐步形成实物工作量，项目配套融
资有望跟进，带动信贷融资边际修复。

风险提示：宏观经济不及预期，地产销售不及预期，美国货币政策超预
期，地缘政治风险超预期。

评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价（或行业
指数）相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。其中 A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准；新三板市场以三板成指（针
对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

Document 028: 2026-10-27_［門間一夫の経済深読み］成長戦略より21世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」を_218ba051

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-27_［門間一夫の経済深読み］成長戦略より21世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」を_218ba051.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-27

### Page 1

1

門間 一夫の 
経済深読み

成長戦略より 21 世紀ニッポンの「 ア
ポロ計画」を 
みずほ総合研究所  
エグゼクティブエコノミスト  門間一夫 2026 年 10 月 27 日

心情的にはノーサレンダー路線 
先月の論考では、①度重なる成長戦略にもかかわらず日本の潜在成長率は低下したこと、②成長戦略
には政策手段の特定が困難という基本問題があるこ と 、③ 25 年英知を尽くしても結果が出なかった事実
は厳粛に受け止めるべきこと、などを述べた 1。 
それを踏まえ、本稿ではあるべき方向性を考えてみたい。選択肢は、(a)25 年で諦めず 50 年でも 100
年でもやる、(b)成長目標を達成しやすい水準に下げる、(c)政府のミッションは成長戦略とは別にある
と考える、の 3 つである。選択肢(b)が無難に見えるが、もともと成長戦略は「現状の成長率ではだめ
だ」という認識に基づくものである。現状を明確に上回る成長率を目指さない限り、成長戦略としては
自己矛盾である。 
すると残るのは (a)ノーサレンダー路線、(c)ミッション再考、のいずれかである。このうちノーサレ
ンダー路線、つまり「成長を諦めない」という不屈の姿勢には人々の共感が得られやすい。しかし、不
屈の志を掲げても、手段の裏付けを欠くという現実は変わらない。手段の裏付けを欠くなら「目標」で
はなく「夢」である。もちろん、夢を語ることが悪いわけではないし、政治家は夢を語るべきという考
えもあるだろう。その夢が偶然にでもかなえば歴史に残る偉業であり、夢なら実現せずとも落胆の度合
いは小さい。ノーサレンダー路線はアップサイドだけでダウンサイドはない、と考えてよいだろうか。 
成長戦略のダウンサイド 
成長戦略にダウンサイドはない、やってみて損はない、と い う 前提は実は正しくない。成長戦略は「た
だ」でできるわけではないからだ。例えば、成長戦略には多かれ少なかれネット財政支出が伴う。国債
をどれだけ発行できるのか議論の余地はあるが、いずれにせよ有限である。 
一方、成長戦略は総花的になりやすい。成長を実現する手段がはっきりしない以上、ある政策案を「 成
長とは無関係」ときっぱり排除するのも難しいからである。経済界の要望や政治家の信念に基づく様々
な政策案が、成長に少しでも関係しそうなら幅広く正当化される。各種施策の費用対効果について一定

1 門間一夫の経済深読み「成長戦略の本質的な限界」（2026 年 9 月 25 日 ）。

### Page 2

2

の分析や議論は行われている。しかし、行われることとそれが有効に機能することは別である。費用対
効果の分析が有効に機能し、各種施策の経済成長へのインパクトが正確に測れていたとしたら、25 年に
わたり成長目標の大幅未達という結果にはならなかったはずである。 
一般に、政策目標の実現に向けてリソースが投入され、目標が実現できなかった場合、そのリソース
投入は失敗だったことになる。チャレンジ自体は無駄ではなかったと言える場合もあるが、過去 25 年
だめだった目標にもう一度挑戦してみるという話なら、たぶん次もだめだろうと事前に予想できる。金
も人もふんだんにあるなら別だが、財政状況が厳しく、人材の不足も叫ばれる国で、高確率で失敗する
ミッションへのリソース投入は賢明な選択と言えるのか。 
有限のリソースが無駄に使われれば、他の課題のために使えるリソースが減る。ここで言うリソース
は財政資金だけではない。実現できない目的のために、多忙な官僚の労力が割かれ、政治家のポリティ
カル・キャピタルも消耗すれば、やればできたはずの他の課題への対応まで中途半端に終わってしまう。
成長戦略には他の課題を犠牲にするという「機会費用」が伴うのであり、それが「ただではない」とい
うことのより正確な意味である。「成長を諦めないノーサレンダー路線でいく」という選択は、「50 年で
も 100 年でもその機会費用を払い続けることを覚悟する」という選択でもある。 
何のための経済成長か 
ここで話を一段階前に戻してみよう。そもそもなぜ、経済成長を高めるべきと考えられているのだろ
うか。もちろん、低成長より高成長の方がいいに決まっているので、経済成長を簡単に高められるので
あれば、そんな疑問を持つ必要はない。しかし、経済成長を高めようとすることで機会費用を払い続け
ることになる確率が高いなら、そうまでしてわれわれが最終的に望んでいるものは何なのかという問い
に、きちんと向き合う必要がある。 
経済成長の必要性を訴える主張は、「〇〇の解決には経済成長が不可欠」「低成長のままなら〇〇のよ
うな不都合が起きる」というパターンにおおむね当てはまるように思う。つまり、現在直面している課
題、あるいは将来直面しそうな課題に対応するには、より高い経済成長が必要という認識が成長戦略の
基礎にあると考えられる。念頭に置かれている課題の中身は、社会保障や財政の持続性、行政・医療・
介護サービスの維持、国際的な存在感の回復、生活水準の向上など、論者により様々である。パイを増
やせるなら改善できる課題は多いので、経済成長を高めるべきという主張は万人受けし、政治家には格
好の政策アジェンダになる。 
しかし、「何らかの課題を経済成長で解決する」という考え方は 、「 その課題に直接向き合うより経済
成長を高める方が簡単だ」という前提がないと成立しない。その前提が間違っていれば、むしろ経済成
長に頼るがゆえに肝心の課題がいつまでも解決しないことになる。重要な課題であればあるほど、経済
成長と切り離して解決する努力を早く始めるべきではないか。 
例えば、社会保障や財政の持続性が課題なのであれば、給付と負担のバランスや、恵まれている人と
そうでない人の公平感を改善することで、問題の解決を目指す方が良い。人々の生活水準を高めたいと
いう課題を経済成長と切り離すのは少し厄介だが、国民全員が等しく困っているわけではなく、問題の

### Page 3

3

本質は経済格差にある。分配政策により、国民生活の満足感や社会の安定性を全体として改善すること
はできる。 
国際社会における日本の存在感を高めることは、経済成長ではそもそも無理であろう。かつて日本が
世界第 2 位の GDP をキープし、経済大国として存在感を示した時代もあった。しかし、あれは冷戦構造
のもとでの日米関係、戦後の高度成長とその余熱、新興国が発展初期段階にあったことなどによる歴史
の偶然であった。GDP の対世界シェアが 3～4％程度まで落ち、今後さらに人口が減り、資源・エネルギ
ーに乏しく、特殊な言語を使う島国が、今から経済成長を多少高められたとしても、そのことで示せる
国際パワーは高が知れている。小さくても存在感がある国をどう作るかが、正しいアジェンダ設定だと
思われる。 
21 世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」は？ 
日本が課題だらけなのは確かだが、25 年間どうやっても達成できなかった経済成長に、その解決をこ
れ以上頼るのが正解とは思いにくい。逆に、優先課題の解決に国が本気でリソースを投入するなら、そ
こに民間も収益機会を見出し、様々な連鎖反応を通じて経済に好影響が及ぶ可能性も出てくる。 
経済学者マリアナ・マッツカートは著書「ミッション・エコノミー」において、大きな変革は市場メ
カニズムだけでは生み出せず、国家が野心的だが実行可能な課題を設定し、民間の力を引き出す仕組み
を築くことが重要と説く 2。マッツカートは代表例として、1960 年代の米国アポロ計画を挙げる。1969
年 7 月 20 日、ニール・アームストロングとバズ・オルドリンが月面に降り立ったことで知られる、あの
アポロ計画である。 
日本の成長戦略も「野心的な課題」を掲げたものではあるが、複雑すぎるマクロ経済そのものを課題
の対象にすると、先月の論考で詳しく述べたように、目的と手段の関係性が特定できない。原因と結果
の関係がよくわからない仕事は、責任の所在があいまいで互いに人任せになり、結果にコミットするこ
との効果が得られない。それに対し、「月に人を送る」などのより具体的なミッションの場合、そのため
には何が必要なのかの因果関係を分析・認識しやすい分、結果にコミットすれば人々の力が正しい方向
へ向かう。 
アポロ計画は、半導体、コンピューター、通信、材料工学などの技術を飛躍的に発展させ、その後の
米国の産業基盤を築いたと言われている。また、計画推進の国家機関であった米航空宇宙局（NASA）は 、
多くの科学技術人材を育成・輩出した。彼らはその後、金融イノベーションなどでも活躍し、NASA 出身
なので「ロケット・サイエンティスト」と呼ばれたことはよく知られている。 
ここで重要なのは、半導体やコンピューターなどの産業育成自体を目的として、政府が補助金で支援
したという話ではないことである。アポロ計画という具体的なミッションがまず存在し、それへの貢献
に適正な対価で報いる仕組みがイノベーターたちを突き動かし、様々な技術や知見が開発、蓄積された
のである。後世に残った産業基盤や人材は、「月に人を送る」というミッションの副産物であった。

2 Mazzucato, Mariana(2021) Mission Economy: A Moonshot Guide to Changing Capitalism, Allen Lane-Penguin 
（関美和・鈴木絵里子訳『ミッション・エコノミー』News Picks、2021 年 ）。

### Page 4

4

一般論として、何らかの課題を解決すれば、経済に必ず大きなプラスの効果が見込める、とまでは言
えない。しかし、上位目標が「課題の解決」なのだから、それが達成された時点でミッションは成功で
あり、投入されたリソースは「有効に使われた」ことになる。そのうえで、マクロ経済にもプラスの波
及効果があればなお良いが、仮にそれがなくても課題が解決された分、以前より望ましい社会になって
いる。 
最大の難関は、優先課題を特定しそれに取り組む社会的モメンタムを生み出すことであり、それには
既存の発想に捉われない強力なビジョンとリーダーシップが要る。アポロ計画にも当初「無謀で財政資
金の無駄使い」という声があった。マッツカートは先ほどの著書の中で、仮に現代風の費用対効果分析
を適用していたら、アポロ計画は採用されず、人類が月面に立つことはなかったと言っている。 
アポロ計画に相当する案件は、どの時代、どの国にも自明なものとしてあるわけではない。「月に人を
送る」というミッションは、当時の米ソ冷戦状況において、ソ連に宇宙開発で打ち勝つことが米国の死
活問題、という歴史特殊的な認識のもとで生まれた。それでも、よく考えてみれば今の日本にも、人口
の減少・高齢化や地政学環境の激変など、人類史上にもまれな歴史特殊的な条件のもとで、「死活問題」
になりつつある課題はいくつかあるように思う。例えば、エネルギーや食料の安定供給、生活インフラ・
医療・介護体制の維持・改善などは、次世代の日本人にとってまさに「死活問題」なのではないか。 
限られたリソースの優先課題への配分が、市場メカニズムで実現できないなら、国が強力に関与しな
ければならない。民間には不得意な国民的課題の解決こそ、国の本来の役割であり、そのためのリソー
スを国債発行でまかないきれないなら、国民に追加負担を求めてもいい。 
言うまでもなくこれは困難な作業である。だから優先課題は何か、そのために国民負担を求めるべき
かなどを深く議論せず、経済成長で様々な問題をまとめて解決できることにしておく方が、実はずっと
楽なのである。しかし、それが問題の先送りにすぎないことは繰り返し述べたとおりである。今も成長
戦略が重要だと言い、政府にそれを求めたがるわれわれの意識は、日本が直面する真の課題やその優先
順位を考えるのは面倒だ、という思考停止から生まれているのかもしれない。21 世紀ニッポンの「アポ
ロ計画」は何なのか、国民的な議論の活性化が必要ではないだろうか。

上週重點訊息
◼ 黃仁勳請辭衝擊輝達，AI 需求韌性支撐科技股反彈：輝達執行長黃仁勳因涉及國安疑慮的相
關爭議，11/12 正式辭任執行長及董事職務，由財務長 Colette Kress 暫代，輝達當日股價盤
中一度重挫近 12%，惟隨後跌幅收斂收跌 4.3%。領導層更迭引發市場對公司策略及 AI 產業
發展的疑慮，但 CoreWeave 第三季營收年增 168%，因受惠 AI 算力需求強勁，此一利多顯
示 AI 需求尚未顯著轉弱，惟輝達管理團隊穩定性、後續財報及政策調查進展，仍是影響科技

股評價的重要變數。
◼ 美國 10 月通膨高於預期，Fed 升息預期推升美債殖利率：美國 11/10 公布 10 月 CPI 月增
0.35%，高於預期的 0.3%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，亦高於預期的 0.2%，顯示通膨壓力仍具黏
性。因 2025 年政府停擺造成資料缺漏，本次未公布年增率。Fed 理事 Waller 表示，若核心
通膨維持 0.3%左右，12 月應考慮升息 2 碼，市場因此提高對後續緊縮的預期。
◼ 伊朗上週末再度對美軍設施進行攻擊，惟美方尚無反擊行動：美國國務卿魯比歐 11/13 表示，
美國對伊朗造成的經濟壓力已大到沒有必要採取軍事行動。不過，伊朗革命衛隊上週末以無人
機攻擊美軍在巴林的雷達站，但美軍尚無進一步反擊，留意之後美方是否仍以封鎖取代攻擊。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短期審慎、中期仍正向，AI 供應鏈基本面持續具支撐：黃仁勳辭任使輝達面臨管理層
接班、企業治理及政策調查等不確定性，加上 Fed 升息預期及 AI 資料中心監管疑慮，市場
短線波動可能延續。然而，該事件並未直接改變 AI 算力需求，且上週 CoreWeave Q3 財報

營收年增 168%、台積電 10 月營收年增 59.7%，均顯示 AI 基礎建設投資仍具動能。整體對
科技股維持短期審慎、但中長期基本面仍在，台灣及日本等具技術競爭優勢、獲利與現金流
穩健的半導體及 AI 供應鏈亦值得留意。惟市場焦點已由資本支出規模轉向投資報酬與現金
流，後續仍須關注輝達財報及管理團隊能否維持客戶信心。
◼ Fed 升息預期壓抑債市，中天期美債及投資級債仍相對具吸引力： 美國 10 月 CPI 高於預
期，加上 Fed 官員偏鷹表態，使債券市場持續承受政策利率上修壓力。美國 10 年期公債殖
利率重回 5%以上，收益水準已具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券雖然價格波動較小，但殖
利率較低，且未來仍面臨政策利率及再投資風險；超長天期債券則易受到財政赤字、發債供
給與期限溢價影響。中天期美債在收益與利率風險之間相對均衡，維持審慎正向看法。信用
債方面，投資級債收益條件仍具支撐，惟須留意高資本支出企業的融資與現金流風險。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%，惟低於第 1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%，薪資增幅續降，顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱。惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%，顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升。另一方面，個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫，未來仍可能進一步升息；惟 John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術。若成真，將擴大台積電美國布局，並支援 Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.5 
一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%，顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩。此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%，致淨出口拖累 GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點。另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%，高於第一季之 1.8%，顯示民間需求明顯增強。物價方面，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%，顯示通膨壓力仍偏高。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人，明顯低於上月修正後的 13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings)由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個，職缺率由 4.4%降至 4.3%，顯示企業用人需求略有降溫；

用相關內容。

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges)人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業；聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯。整體而言，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由 53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由 51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由 51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由 53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%；扣除食品及能源後之核心 PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

### Page 7

用相關內容。

6

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標，未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性；惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示「目前僅在討論階
段，但可能會有進展」。目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權，台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨、核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%，主因國際原油價格上漲，使能源通膨上升 4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%，失業人數增加 2.6 萬人，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過，基於 PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%，較第一季降 0.6 個百分點，主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

用相關內容。

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算，也可能面臨倒閣風險；此外，市場對於 2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑 6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長，加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期 9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限，反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長 
(1)新加坡：受服務業、零售暨其他商品業，以及食品價格漲幅擴大影響，8 月消

條件不佳影響，預期新加坡進口食品、商品及服務成本將持續上升，惟國內生產
率成長及政府補貼政策將抑制部分服務業通膨，新加坡金管局(MAS)或在 10 月
「略微」緊縮貨幣政策。(2)泰國：8 月出口額年增 24.3%(346 億美元)，主要由工

創兩年新高，主要得益於電子產品（年增 59.9%），尤其是半導體（73.5%）及黃

2026 年 11 月 17 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）結果

～個人消費が力強さを欠き、4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表した 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比－0.0%（年率換算   
－0.1%）と減少した。設備投資が前期比＋1.6%と高めの伸びとなった一方、個人消費が同－0.3%と 2 四半期連
続で減少したことなどが下押し要因となり、小幅ながら 4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。なお、輸入が鉱物
性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスとなったが、公的在庫の寄与
度が＋0.5%ポイントとなり、おおむね相殺された。 
過去 3 四半期にわたって潜在成長率を上回る高めの伸びが続いていたことや、設備投資が堅調に増加したこ
とを踏まえると、今回のマイナス成長をもって景気回復の動きが途切れたとは判断できないが、景気が力強さを欠
いていることも事実だろう。特に GDP の過半を占める個人消費に弱さがみられたことは懸念材料である。今後は、
良好な雇用・所得環境に支えられて個人消費は持ち直しへ向かうと期待されるが、消費者物価の伸び拡大や住
宅ローン金利の上昇による家計負担の増加を通じて、消費の減少が続くリスクには警戒が必要である。 
なお、物価上昇分を含んだ名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.4%（年率換算＋1.6%）と、10 四半期連続のプラス成長
となった。物価上昇圧力は依然として強く、コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比  
＋2.4%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.4%）と、高い伸びが続いた。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度（%ポイント）

 1 次速報値を踏まえた、2026 年 7～9 月期における需要項目別の動向（実質ベース）は以下の通り。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.3%） 
 家計を取り巻く所得環境は改善した（実質雇用者報酬は前期比＋0.3%）が、前期に自動車や
エアコンが一時的な要因によって増加した反動で耐久財が前期比－3.5%と減少した上、夏場
の地震や台風、長雨などの自然災害・天候不順によって消費の機会に恵まれなかったこと、食
料品の値上げや住宅ローン金利の上昇など今後の生活不安につながる話題も多く節約志向が
強まったことから、半耐久財や非耐久財も減少した。ただし、サービスは 9 月のシルバーウイー
クの日並びが良かったこともあり、小幅に増加した。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が 2 四半期ぶりに減少する中、建築コストの増加も逆風とな
り、住宅投資は 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋1.6%） 
 企業の省力化や生産性向上に向けた投資意欲は強く、特に 7～9 月期はサーバーや半導体製
造装置といった AI 関連投資も好調だったことで、設備投資は 3 四半期ぶりに増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度＋0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に大きめのプラス寄与となったこともあり、民間在庫の寄与は横ばいとなった。 
 なお、公的在庫は国家備蓄原油の減少幅が縮小したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントと
なった。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費（前期比－0.0%） 
 前期に高めの伸びとなったこともあり、横ばいだった。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－1.1%） 
 名目では増加したものの、工事コストの増加を受けたデフレーターの上昇に追いついておらず、
実質では 2 四半期連続で減少した。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.6%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.2%、実質輸入：同＋2.5%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.2%と減少した。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受けて増加し
た一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動で減少した。 
 輸入は前期比＋2.5%と増加した。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただし、サービス
については減少した。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.6%ポイントと大きめのマイナスになった。

Inventec (2356.TW)

Inventec
Company description
Established in 1975 and listed in 1996, Taiwan-based Inventec is a key ODM
of NBs, servers, smartphones, and wearable/IoT devices to global customers.
Major shareholders include the founder Mr. Yeh Kuo-I, whose family has (both
directly and indirectly) a c35% stake.
Investment strategy
We rate Inventec shares as Buy. We expect stable demand in NB and server to
help support near-term earnings, while product diversification within smart

devices to areas such as IoT and medical space should help improve L-T
margins. We expect the company's solid cash yield to serve as a downside
cushion.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$65 for Inventec shares is based on 17x 2027 EPS, which
is slightly above the mid-point of its historical trading range, reflecting the
company's product diversification and stable demand outlook led by servers
and commercial NBs, as well as increasing AI server exposure that could

potentially drive margin expansion and better earnings momentum.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) Slower-than-expected shipment growth/weaker-than-expected
sell-through in HomePods and AirPods could lead to downside in our sales
forecasts; 2) If competitors adopt aggressive pricing strategies to gain market
shares, our ASP and margin assumptions could face downside risk; 3) Lower-
than-expected server margins driven by rising exposure to Chinese customers

could impact our earnings forecasts negatively; 4) Deterioration in NB
demand and investment in solar would hurt our forecasts; and 5) Stronger-
than-expected Taiwan dollar could lead to FX loss and downside to our
current forecasts.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Idea
See our 3Q26 results recap: 3Q26 Results Summary
4Q preview – revenue growth and margin expansion: We estimate 4Q revenue
will rise ~8% q/q, to NT$31.8bn (+94% y/y), driven by higher ASPs and shipments
deferred from Sep – Oct following flood-related logistics disruptions in Thailand.
We expect sequential revenue growth across all major product segments except PC/
NB, with networking delivering the largest increase, followed by AI/ASIC servers and

general servers. We forecast margin expansion of ~100bps q/q, to 39.7% (+600bps
y/y), supported by continued mix improvement and pricing adjustments to pass
through higher copper-clad laminate (CCL) costs. This should translate into net
profit of ~NT$7bn (+9% q/q, +130% y/y) and EPS of NT$13.82.
GCE’s Suzhou 2 and Thailand 2 plants remain on track to begin mass production
(MP) from 3Q27 and reach full ramp-up by end-CY27. Both facilities will support

advanced high-density interconnect (HDI) production for a key customer’s next-gen
compute board, which we think could adopt a 34-layer (3+28+3) advanced HDI
design using M9-grade CCL. While details remain subject to finalization, we expect
each plant to add monthly output value of ~NT$1bn or more. We also expect at
least one additional plant to begin advanced HDI MP in CY28, although details have
yet to be finalized. This would give GCE at least three plants capable of advanced

HDI production to meet demand from existing ASIC, and potential new, customers.
Reiterate OW with PT slightly lowered to NT$1,400, on our reduced medium-term
growth assumption (from 12% to 11%), as we bake in some conservatism on
concerns over a potential slowdown in data center build-out post the US midterm
election (see our Global Team's view), despite our marginally higher EPS estimates
for 2026-28e. Our new PT implies ~19x 2027e P/E, or ~14x 2028e P/E, slightly

lowered but still above GCE's 3Y P/E avg. of ~14x, which we view as fair, driven by its
capacity expansion plans and strong demand for AI servers and networking, with
room for margin expansion.

4
3Q26 Results Summary
• GCE reported 3Q26 gross margin of 38.7% (+400bp q/q, +310bp y/y), 180bp above
MSe and 190bps above the Street forecast. Management attributed the gross
margin improvement to successful cost pass-through, more favorable product mix
(AI mix was over 50% in the quarter; 800G switch mix also continued to increase
within Networking) and better profitability of the Thailand plant.
• Recall that we noted although GCE is able to fully pass through the additional

material costs to its customers, there will always be a timing difference (GCE
seeing higher input costs vs. reflecting the higher costs in new prices), which might
result in margin fluctuation.
• Operating expenses were again higher than expected, primarily due to higher
employee bonuses for talent retention and sales expenses. Thus, operating profit
came in at NT$8.8B (+36% q/q, +88% y/y), in line with MSe but 1% below
consensus forecasts, for operating margin of 29.6%.

• Non-operating losses of NT$238mn, primarily consisting of FX losses, were higher
than our and Street estimates of non-operating gains.
• Net profit was NT$6.4B (+33% q/q, +98% y/y), 2% above our estimate and    the
Street, for EPS of NT$12.39.
• The differences between net profit variances and EPS variances reflect share count
changes.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
We lower our price target slightly, to NT$1,400.00 from NT$1,450.00: We derive our
price target, which is also our base-case scenario value, from a residual income (RI) model,
similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage. The decrease in our price target is
driven by our slightly lowered medium-term growth assumption.
Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of

8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 11% (lowered from 12%)
and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies ~19x 2027e P/E, or ~14x 2028e P/E, vs. GCE's three-year historical
average 14.2x. We view the higher multiple as justified because GCE is showing longer-
term margin expansion, driven by a favorable mix shift toward more AI server PCBs and
high-speed networking PCBs (400G/800G/1.6T) alike. Some of its AI component peers are

trading at 20x+ 2027e multiple, which leads us to view GCE's valuation as fair.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values decrease at  magnitudes similar to the decline
in our base-case value, to NT$1,995.00 and NT$850.00, respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
11 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Auras Technology
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling
market of AI servers. Our TP of NT$2,000 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the company’s
expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates,
CDMs, and CDUs.

Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported in our view by
other qualitative factors such as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

All Ring Tech Co. (6187.TWO): CoWoS expansion to drive growth ahead; 
Oct rev on track; 3Q26 OP income in line; Buy
12 November 2026 | 7:57AM HKT
Oct revenues on track: Oct revenues came in at NT$978mn, +218% YoY but -16% 
MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +41% QoQ), accounting for 33% of our 4Q26E revenue 
forecast. Looking into 4Q26, we expect revenue to decline 11% QoQ, yet 24% ahead 
of our prior forecast on stronger-than-expected CoWoS related demand, while we 
lower our GM assumption to 50.6% (vs. 52.6% previously) to reflect a less favorable 
product mix and early stage shipments of new tools for panel level packaging and 
CPO. For 2027, we expect continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and 
OSAT customers, rising content value in panel level packaging and the ramp of the 
new CPO product line to drive growth, with GM set to recover as new products reach 
scale. Maintain Buy.

3Q26 operating income in line: Revenue was pre-announced. GM of 48.5% came in 
below GSe/Bloomberg consensus of 54.9%/54.3%, which we attribute to product 
mix, with a higher contribution from lower-margin automation equipment. However, 
with a larger revenue scale and a lower opex ratio of 15.6% (vs. GSe/cons. of 
18.2%/17.7%), operating income grew 39% QoQ, 2% / 1% behind GSe/cons., largely 
in line. Below the operating line, All Ring booked a non-operating loss of NT$55mn in 
3Q26 (vs. non-operating income of NT$174mn in 2Q26), primarily attributable to 
valuation losses from equity investment, partly reversing the gains booked in 2Q26, 
which led EPS (NT$8.49) to miss by 10% vs GSe.

### Page 2

CoWoS expansion and new product ramps to drive growth: We expect 4Q26E 
revenue to decline 11% QoQ, still well ahead of our prior forecast on CoWoS-related 
demand. Our forecast implies average monthly revenue of NT$983mn in 
November-December (broadly flat vs. October). On the margin front, we expect GM to 
remain subject to product mix, with new tools carrying lower margins in the initial ramp. 
Longer term, we remain positive on All Ring, and expect 2027-28E growth to be driven 
by 1) continued CoWoS capacity expansion at both foundry and OSAT customers; 2) 
panel-level packaging, which brings higher-ASP tools and additional process steps, 
lifting content value per line; 3) the ramp of its new CPO product line, spanning fiber 
coupling and optical engine inspection tools, which we expect to start contributing 
meaningfully in 2027; and 4) new customer penetration, including a US customer’s 
advanced packaging capacity.

Earnings revision: We incorporate the 3Q26 results into our model and revise our 
2026/2027/2028E EPS by +2.7%/0.0%/+1.7%, as 1) higher revenue 
(+9.4%/+1.4%/+3.6% vs. our prior forecasts) on stronger CoWoS-related demand and 2) 
a lower opex ratio on the larger revenue base are offset by 3) lower GM, reflecting the 
product mix and the initial ramp of new products, and 4) the non-operating loss in 
3Q26. Net net, we expect All Ring’s revenue to grow 87%/33%/29% YoY in 2026-28E 
(vs. 71%/44%/26% previously), with GM of 51.0%/53.1%/53.5% (vs. 
53.8%/54.2%/54.9% previously).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

### Page 1

King Slide

Buy: strong 3Q26 results, 88.2% GM; solid 
’27/28E sales/margin; cut PO to NT$12,988

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 12,988 TWD | Price: 8,165 TWD

3Q26 results: another impressive beat with 88.2% GM 
King Slide’s 3Q26 GM/OPM hit record highs of 88.2%/82.9% for the fourth/second 
consecutive quarter, respectively, well above BofAe of 83.7%/78.2% and consensus of 
84.0%/78.6%. OP (+62% QoQ, +383% YoY) was 7%/5% above BofAe/consensus. We 
attribute the solid margins/results to sizable sales scale (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) and 
better operating efficiency. With non-operating gain (mainly net interest income), NP 
(+65% QoQ, +266% YoY) was 8%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3). 
BofAe 4Q26 sales -20% QoQ; solid 2027/28 sales/margin 
We now expect King Slide’s 4Q26 sales to decline 20% QoQ, from the high 3Q26 base 
and amid GPU platform transition. We believe Sep/Oct sales (-31%/+2% MoM) shall 
serve as evidence for slower order momentum. Our 4Q26 sales forecast implies flat-to-
slightly higher MoM sales in Nov/Dec. We now expect another 45%/20% YoY sales 
growth in 2027/28, with stronger pull-in demand resuming in 1Q27. In our view, 
2027/28 sales should be supported by various AI projects across platforms (GPU/ASIC/ 
AMD) and clients (global/regional CSPs, neoclouds, AI start-ups, enterprises). We also 
keep monitoring the capacity ramp-up from the new US Houston site, which may bring 
further sales upside. We expect high and sustainable GM at 85.0% both in 2027/28. We 
see FX as a potential swing factor to GM, given the company’s natural-hedging policy.  
Trim PO to NT$12,988: lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lower our 2026-27E EPS by 0-4% but lift our 2028E EPS by 7%, mainly to reflect 
3Q26 beat and stronger margins. Yet, we cut PO to NT$12,988 (28x 2027E EPS) from 
NT$14,888 (32x 2027E EPS), with valuation base unchanged but a lower P/E multiple. 
We now use 28x (2SD above average since IPO) on valuation de-rating across AI supply 
chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on King Slide in view of its 
lion share in AI server rail market, diversified client/project base, strong margin profile.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
King Slide is the global top server rail kit manufacturer from 
Taiwan. It also supplies slide rail kit for kitchen/furniture. Its 
key server clients include global cloud service providers and 
enterprise servers.

King Slide was founded in 1986 and was publicly listed on 
Taiwan Stock Exchange on 25 June 2008. Its headquarter 
and main production sites are located in Kaohsiung 
(Taiwan). It also has capacity in Houston (US).

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to King Slide given its leading 
position in server rail to ride on strong AI market demand 
with rising content value thanks to increasing component 
density and customization. We highlight King Slide's early 
step into server market since 2001 with proven track 
record, much larger scale, time-to-market capability, higher 
automation level, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for 
high/above-peer margins.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$12,988 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. We use 28x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading position in 
server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. Its 
early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much larger scale 
than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Lotes’s 3Q26 results, released on 13 November.

What’s the impact: Business outlook. Ongoing gross margin dilution due 
to rising raw material costs and less benefits from the AI server trend has 
been, in our view, the primary drag on Lotes’s share price over this year. 
We believe this trend may reverse now as investors are worried about the 
potential AI capex cuts impacting the overall Taiwan supply chain’s revenue 
outlook in 2028, whereas Lotes only has 3-5% of exposure currently. Also, 
management expects gross margin pressure to start easing with the new 
product mass production (eg, SOCAMM) starting from 4Q26. Second, 
Lotes’s regular server-related products may see less chance for inventory 
correction as its products are not in shortage now. Lastly, its desktop 
business has suffered more than the overall industry’s headwinds (ie, unit 
decline 10-15% YoY) as its desktop products had to downgrade from DDR5 
to DDR4/3 due to component shortages, which further negatively impacted 
profitability. We believe these three headwinds will gradually transform into 
tailwinds once the AI capex starts to slow down. However, if the AI server 
capex trend remains as is, we could also see some upside for the 
company. Lotes is still eager to develop a GPU socket for the Rubin Ultra 
generation, which we believe could become a new growth driver. Besides, 
quick disconnect (QD) and NPO sockets might also become growth drivers. 
While QD only accounts for low single digit of total revenue from 3Q26, we 
expect it to have a chance to increase to c.10% of total revenue in 2027E.

3Q26 profitability missed expectations. Lotes’s 3Q26 earnings came in 
at TWD1.92bn (EPS: TWD17.1), which was 11%/14% lower than our/street 
estimates. Despite revenue largely in-line with expectations, gross margin 
decreased by 0.6pp QoQ to 44.8%, mainly from rising copper and gold 
prices; unfavourable product mix (rising high-speed cable and connect and 
QD) may be another reason. However, operating margin rose 0.9pp QoQ to 
22.9% mainly on the lack of one-off employee stock bonus expense (as 
done in 2Q26) but opex was still higher than the company’s guidance 
(<TWD2.0bn) due to salary increase for its Chinese branches.

What we recommend: We lower 2026-27E EPS by 2-7% to factor in 3Q26 
results and lower profitability recovery assumption but maintain 2028E 
EPS. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call but lower our 12M TP to TWD2,480 (from 
TWD2,850), based on a lower PER of 22x (from 28x; high end of its past-5-
year range of 10-28x), on our 1-year forward EPS, given the weaker market 
sentiment. Key risk: lower-than-expected adoption of server CPU platforms.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E revenues are 5-10% higher than the 
Bloomberg consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on QDs.

13 November 2026

➢ Rising QD, SOCAMM, NPO sockets to be key growth drivers in 2027

Company profile 
Founded in 1986 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2007, Lotes is one of the world’s leading 
connector vendors specialising in CPU socket/ILM and connectors, such as DDR, PCIe, SATA, and 
Type-C, with most of its production capacities at its Guangzhou, Zhongshan and Suzhou plants. Its 
superior manufacturing capability and strong client relationships are the key differentiators that make 
Lotes stand out from its competitors. Since the PC market became saturated from 2015, Lotes has 
focused on penetrating non-PC connector businesses, such as connectors for servers, automotive and 
industrial, and new types of cables (eg, Thunderbolt cable).

### Page 5

5

Lotes (3533 TT): 13 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 13 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Lotes’ board consists of 7 directors, including 3 independent directors. The total number of
directors is slightly lower than other listed companies in our coverage. Lotes held 8 board
meetings in 2025 and the actual attendance rate was 95%.
Although the Chairman and CEO are not the same person, they are, in fact, brothers and the co-
founders of Lotes. It gives us a sense that Lotes is still a family enterprise, which is likely a
negative for the board and executive management. That said, the background of independent
directors is diversified with expertise in technology and management. We see it as a positive for
the board's quality.
Capital management 2
Lotes declared a DPS of TWD41.5 in 2025, representing a payout ratio of 50%. Being a leading
connector vendor for applications such as desktops, notebooks and servers, Lotes grew rapidly
along with its key clients (Intel and AMD) on strong technical capabilities and higher growth rate
from the server market. The solid revenue CAGR in the past decade required higher working
capital and continuous capex to expand its capacity, which could also be the culprit for its slightly
lower payout ratio compared with other electronic component makers. Having said that, we think
Lotes’ higher revenue growth should partially offset the drawback of a lower payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1
In 2025, there were no disclosed related-party sales, while purchases from related parties
amounted to TWD4.4m, representing 0.03% of total cost of sales. We see limited risk from
related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
Lotes established its RBA management system in 2010 and requires its suppliers to meet the
standards of no child labour, no forced labour, treating employees with respect and dignity, and
caring for labourers' safety and health. Lotes hosts a yearly supplier conference to promote the
RBA guidelines to its suppliers. In 2025, 187 suppliers participated in the annual supplier
conference. Lotes also requires its suppliers to sign the RBA-related commitment letters. For
better supply chain management, Lotes conducts yearly audits and regular business reviews
and holds the yearly supplier conference as measures. In 2025, Lotes conducted written
evaluations of 110 suppliers and on-site audits of 61 suppliers. In addition, all 76 newly added
suppliers passed its environmental and social responsibility evaluations. Lotes has required new
suppliers to sign the "Supplier Social and Environmental Responsibility Commitment Letter"
since 2010 to extend social responsibility concepts and requirements to the supply chain.
For "green" purchase management, Lotes has adopted the IECQ QC 080000 hazardous
substance process management system. Lotes asks its suppliers to follow the RoHS, HSF, and
REACH guidelines to avoid using hazardous materials. Lotes also conducts conflict minerals due
diligence and requires suppliers to sign non-conflict mineral declarations and complete surveys
tracing mineral sources to smelters. Overall, Lotes believes it has constructed a comprehensive
system covering system certification (e.g., ISO 9001), product design, supply chain
management, and production techniques to ensure that the entire manufacturing process, from
raw materials to end products, follows green principles. All products from Lotes are now 100%
compliant with RoHS standards.
S Data security 2
Lotes has established a team to implement data security regulations, planning and maintenance.
Moreover, it monitors vulnerabilities in its information systems each year through regular
vulnerability scanning, access control reviews and information security audits. The company
reported no losses resulting from information security incidents in 2025.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Document 253: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점삼성바이오로직스(207940) 불확실성보다 성장을 볼 시점불확실성보다 성장_3801b631.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
3분기 실적 컨센서스 상회 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 매출액은 1조 4,903억 원(+18.5% YoY), 
영업이익은 6,401억 원(+1.0% YoY, 영업이익률 43.0%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 각각 
10%, 5% 상회했다. 1~4공장의 풀가동이 이어진 가운데 5공장의 인증용 생산배치 
물량과 미국 락빌 공장 매출(약 1,000억 원)이 처음으로 반영되며 외형 성장을 
견인했다. 매출원가율은 50.4%로 전년 동기(41.4%), 전분기(47.0%) 대비 
상승했는데, 락빌 공장과 5공장의 초기 물량 반영에 따른 것으로 판단한다. 향후 
물량 상승에 따라 매출원가율은 감소할 것으로 예상한다. 그럼에도 EBITDA 
마진율은 50.4%로 높은 수준을 유지하며 7,515억 원을 달성했다. 순이익은 원/달러 
환율 하락에 따른 외화환산손실이 반영되며 3,911억 원을 기록했다.  
환율 하락에도 가이던스 상향하며 성장 궤도 유지 
2026년 연간 매출액은 5조 6,133억 원(+23.2% YoY), 영업이익은 2조 4,605억 
원(+18.9% YoY)으로 전망한다. 최근 원/달러 환율 하락에도 이번 실적발표에서 
상향 제시한 연간 매출 성장률 20% 이상은 충분히 달성 가능할 것으로 판단한다. 
4분기에는 락빌 공장의 매출 약 1,000억 원과 5공장 가동 확대에 더해, 5월 
파업으로 이연된 물량의 매출 인식이 성장에 기여할 전망이다.  
투자의견 매수 및 목표주가 2,000,000원 유지 
비만 치료제 CDMO 진출을 위한 폴리펩타이드 그룹 인수는 공개매수 응모율 
87.88%로 최소 조건을 충족해 11월 말 완료를 앞두고 있어 신규 성장 동력은 
확보된 것으로 판단한다. 노사 리스크도 법원의 쟁의행위 제한 확대에 이어 노조가 
2차 총파업을 잠정 연기하며 크게 완화됐다. 미국 관세 불확실성으로 부진했던 신규 
수주 모멘텀은 올해 연말부터 내년에 걸쳐 강화될 것으로 전망하며, 불확실성 
해소와 성장 기대감을 반영할 시점이라 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

Topics  Regulatory reform in India's insurance sector

Over the past quarter century, the Indian government has pursued the liberalization of the country's insurance market, 
with the aim of bringing stability to people's living standards and strengthening financial intermediation functions. 
Recently, however, it has begun to reverse course, tightening rules in order to better protect consumers.

■ India's insurance market has grown rapidly with deregulation 
The Indian government has long pursued 
the development of the country's insurance 
market, with the aim of bringing stability to 
people's living standards and strengthening 
financial intermediation functions. This 
stance can be attributed to two background 
factors:  
The first is that India's social security 
system remains underdeveloped. As a result, 
enrollment in health and life insurance can 
provide a vital safety net for mitigating the 
risk of sharp declines in household income 
due to unexpected illnesses or accidents.  
The second is that the sustainable 
development of the Indian economy requires 
the accumulation of long-term and stable 
domestic capital, separate from short-term 
bank deposits, in order to finance the 
development of infrastructure and the 
nurturing of industry. The hope has been that 
the enormous sums of money collected and 
managed by insurance companies can serve 
as a reliable source for this long-term 
financing.  
These factors have led the Indian 
government to walk a path of gradual 
deregulation over the past quarter century. 
The process began with the liberalization of 
market entry by private and foreign 
companies in 2000, which was followed by 
a lifting of the ban on "bancassurance" (the 
sale of insurance through bank branches) in 
2002, the raising of the ceiling on foreign 
ownership, and other reforms.  
This series of deregulatory measures 
spurred the entry of numerous private and foreign 
firms, causing market competition to intensify. Several 
Japanese insurers, including Nippon Life and the Tokio 
Marine Group, also entered the market, helping to drive 
investment in India.  
Besides deregulation related to market entry and 
sales, other factors also contributed to the rapid growth 
of India's insurance market, namely the expansion of 
the middle class, improvements in household financial 
literacy, and the digitalization of insurance services. 
Premium income grew at a pace exceeding economic 
growth, and in recent years insurance companies' assets 
under management have reached a level equivalent to 
Year Event
Establishment of the IRDAI (Insurance Regulatory
and Development Authority of India)
Acceptance of market entry applications from
domestic private firms and foreign entities
(foreign equity cap set at 26%)

3 
incentives to sell products and inappropriate sales practices. This should shore up the credibility of the 
insurance market and the foundation for its sustainable development. 
On the minus side, there are worries about side effects arising from the transition. As rules on selling 
become stricter, insurance companies and the financial institutions acting as insurance agents will inevitably 
see some temporary moderation in the pace of their earnings growth. Moreover, given that insurance 
companies and financial institutions are major investors in India's financial markets, tighter regulation is 
likely to exert downward pressure on equity and bond prices. 
These changes in the insurance market provide an important perspective for assessing the future 
trajectory of the Indian economy. They illustrate that rapid market expansion driven by liberalization can 
create a variety of problems. And this dynamic is not limited to the insurance sector, as it can also occur 
across many other industries. At the same time, the redesign and recalibration of regulations in response to 
such developments can provide a foundation for a new stage of growth. The new insurance regulations 
could constitute an early example of this phenomenon, so will be closely watched to see what changes they 
bring in the years ahead.  
  (Shotaro Kumagai)

Topics  Talent travel curbs may weigh on China’s growth

In September, the Chinese government introduced new exit-entry rules. Indicative of an effort to retain talent in order 
to prevent technology leakage, the rules could narrow opportunities for China to absorb overseas knowledge and weigh 
on its economic growth over the medium to long term.

■ Scope of overseas travel restrictions expanded 
On September 15, the Chinese 
government brought in new rules 
specifying conditions and screening 
procedures for entry and exit. The new 
rules explicitly state that the Ministry of 
Commerce or other departments may 
prohibit Chinese citizens from leaving the 
country if they violate export-control or 
technology import/export regulations and 
are deemed likely to threaten national 
industrial or technological security.  
Under China's Exit and Entry 
Administration Law, which came into 
effect in 2013, defendants and suspects in 
criminal cases, as well as persons involved 
in unsettled civil cases whom courts have 
prohibited from leaving the country, had 
already been subject to exit restrictions. In 
addition, Chinese citizens could be denied 
permission to leave the country if the 
competent departments determined that 
their departure might endanger national 
security or interests. Aside from formal exit 
bans, personnel who handle state secrets 
and officials in the public sector have long 
been subject to controls such as centralized 
passport management and the need to 
obtain approval in advance of overseas travel. What the new rules effectively do is make it clear that exit 
can also be restricted when there are issues from the standpoint of economic security. 
Against this backdrop, it was reported at the end of September that overseas travel restrictions already 
imposed on key personnel in the AI and semiconductor sectors had been expanded to include their spouses, 
children, and other family members. According to these reports, these key personnel include prominent AI 
startup founders, senior executives at major private enterprises, and core R&D talent. Although the measure 
does not constitute a blanket travel ban, affected individuals and their families are reportedly required to 
obtain government approval for exit even in the case of short private trips or business travel. 
This regulatory tightening is thought to be related to the technological rivalry between the U.S. and China. 
As competition surrounding critical technologies such as generative AI intensifies, the Chinese government 
appears to view not only technologies themselves, but also the researchers and engineers who create them, 
as strategic assets. By widening travel curbs to encompass family members, the authorities are likely 
seeking to prevent overseas emigration by individuals or the relocation of R&D operations abroad. A 
noteworthy aspect of the recent reports is that the controls apparently now extend beyond the public sector 
to key personnel at private enterprises, as well as their families. Restrictions like these on talent mobility 
could undermine the mechanisms of technology absorption and productivity improvement that have 
hitherto facilitated China's growth.

■ China's productivity gains have been supported by market opening and technology 
absorption 
Productivity exerts a substantial influence on 
economic growth. Total factor productivity (TFP), an 
indicator of an economy's overall production 
efficiency, provides an estimate of efficiency in 
production that cannot be explained solely in terms of 
inputs of capital and labor, and reflects factors such as 
levels of technology and efficiency of resource 
allocation. 
China's TFP has been rising for many years. In 
addition to the transition to a market economy and the 
growth of private enterprises since the country adopted 
the policy of reform and opening up, Chinese 
productivity has benefitted from the absorption of 
foreign technology and managerial know‑how through 
trade and direct investment. A labor shift from 
agriculture to manufacturing and other sectors, along 
with competition among firms and the weeding out of 
weaker players, has also helped deliver productivity 
gains across the economy.  
Nevertheless, China's TFP remains low relative to 
other major economies. According to estimates from 
Penn World Table, China's TFP in 2023 was less than 
half that of the U.S., and it was also below the levels not 
only of Germany and other major European economies 
and of South Korea and Japan, but of Thailand and 
Malaysia, too. Factors behind this include inefficient 
resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced 
technologies and management practices. Preferential 
financial and institutional treatment for state‑owned 
enterprises, regional protectionism, and mechanisms 
that impede market entry and exit have distorted the 
allocation of resources, preventing capital and labor 
from being fully utilized by more productive firms and sectors. Moreover, although China has achieved 
strong competitiveness in certain advanced industries, such as electric vehicles and semiconductors, the 
leading-edge technologies and management practices developed in these sectors have not sufficiently 
diffused to the service sector and small and medium‑sized enterprises, which form the foundation of the 
economy, and this has constrained overall productivity growth.  
■ Moves to contain technology could hinder growth over the medium to long term 
Efforts to retain talent in order to prevent technology leakage could further delay China's productivity 
catch‑up with advanced economies. While restrictions on overseas travel by elite professionals may help 
with the retention of domestic R&D talent and protection of current technological advantages, they could 
also reduce opportunities to absorb overseas knowledge and to jointly develop new technologies with 
foreign partners. The transfer of technology and know‑how does not occur solely through the sharing of 
"explicit knowledge," in the form of academic papers, technical blueprints, and so on. "Tacit knowledge," 
which is transmitted through face‑to‑face discussion, collaborative work, and the acquisition of experience 
in production settings, is also crucial. If travel controls delay or prevent participation in international 
conferences, joint research projects, and technology exchanges with overseas companies, opportunities to 
absorb such knowledge may be lost, slowing technological innovation and improvements in production 
processes. Furthermore, if obtaining travel approval becomes more time‑consuming or less predictable, the 
overseas assignment or international deployment of technical personnel could also be obstructed. 
The new rules also cover the entry of foreign nationals. If uncertainty surrounding the administration of 
entry and exit controls increases, it could create obstacles to attracting and retaining highly skilled talent 
from abroad. At a time when inefficient resource allocation and the slow diffusion of advanced knowledge 
are already weighing on productivity growth in China, curbs on people-to- people exchange could further

impede both the absorption and creation of knowledge, as well as its practical application. Although the 
magnitude of the impact will depend on the scope of application and actual implementation on the ground, 
restrictions on talent exchanges and overseas travel that reflect excessive emphasis on technology 
protection could slow China's TFP growth over the medium to long term and act as a headwind to the 
Chinese economy by lowering its potential growth rate. 
(Hiromu Komiya)

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

FRT 148.70 1.2% 143.40 165.00 15.1% 
- Sau khi phủ sóng các thành phố lớn, chuỗi Long Châu đang mở rộng sang các vùng cấp 2, 3. 
Năm 2026 dự kiến mở thêm 400-450 cửa hàng (+20%YoY) với DT trên mỗi cửa hàng cũ tăng 
trưởng 6%-8%/năm 
- Dư địa lấy thêm thị phần từ các cửa hàng nhỏ lẻ vẫn còn tương đối rộng, FRT đặt mục tiêu 
chiếm 50% thị phần doanh thu ngành bán lẻ dược phẩm, từ mức 17%-20% hiện tại 
- Tiềm năng dài hạn từ mảng tiêm chủng tương đối tích cực 
FRT

cấp đồ điện tử, chu kỳ Elnino và nhu cầu BĐS bàn giao 
- Số lượng cửa hàng mở mới của BHX có thể đạt 1000 cửa hàng trong 2026 (+25% YoY) với tăng

sẽ tỷ lệ thuận với quy mô DT

• India’s CPI could bump up on higher food costs 
• Taiwan’s exports could moderate as the AI cycle normalizes

### Page 2

The Week Ahead November 6, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Taiwan

Export growth is expected to ease to 
44.4% yoy in October from 60.9% in 
September. A higher comparison base 
makes exceptional export growth 
difficult to sustain from 4Q onwards. The 
global AI cycle is also expected to settle 
into a more normal pace into 2027, as 
higher interest rates lift capital costs 
and US mid-term election results 
heighten regulatory uncertainty. These 
factors underpin our forecast for 
Taiwan’s GDP growth to return to high 
single digits from 4Q onwards and into 
2027.

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s industrial 
production growth to rise to 5.5% yoy in 
September 2026 from 5.0% yoy in 
August. The uptick was likely driven by 
export-oriented manufacturing as 
strong artificial intelligence (AI)-related 
demand continued to support growth in 
electrical and electronics goods, 
alongside a smaller contraction in mining.

China

Export growth is expected to decelerate 
from 36.3% yoy in September to 28.1% in 
October. Both the official manufacturing 
PMI and new export orders sub-index fell 
below 50 after two months in expansion 
territory. High-frequency trade indicators 
also point to slowing momentum, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage handled 
at China’s 20 major ports falling from 7.2% 
yoy in September to -4.2% in October. 
Freight activity weakened as well, with 
growth in international cargo flights 
dropping from 3.6% yoy to -5.9% over the 
same period. On the price front, factory-gate 
and raw material price pressures moderated, 
with the main raw material purchasing price 
and producer price sub-indices declining 
from 60.8 and 54.0 to 56.8 and 51.7, 
respectively. Despite remaining above the 
50 threshold, the moderation suggests some 
easing in pipeline price pressures. 
Downstream consumer price pressures are 
also likely to remain subdued amid soft 
domestic demand. As a result, CPI inflation 
is expected to ease slightly from 0.9% yoy in 
September to 0.8% in October.

India

We expect headline inflation to rise to 6.0% 
yoy in October from 5.5% the month before, 
on higher food costs and a low base effect. 
High frequency data pointed to a further 
pick-up in sequential pressures in food 
segments, across cereals, edible oil, 
perishables (vegetables) etc. Non-food 
components should find relief from further 
moderation in precious metals and delay in 
passage of retail fuel price changes. Core 
inflation is nonetheless set to quicken to 4.5-
4.6% as services notch increases and as 
indirect tax effects in the base, fall out of the 
equation. With inflation expected to test 
past the higher end of the RBI target range, 
we expect a second successive rate hike in 
December. The decline in oil prices has also 
led to cooling of overnight OIS rates as 
concerns over aggressive rate hikes have 
eased. Goods trade deficit is likely to stay 
wide at above $30bn in Oct.

Executive summary
接近年末，市場都會棄舊迎新尋找明年飆股及本夢⽐⾏情，主要是獲利有⼤幅
增⻑潛⼒的公司都會在獲利還沒爆發前提前先跑，⽽造成本益⽐爆表，尤其投
資⼈在經過  9-10 ⽉份全球科技及半導體產業股價從歷史⾼點修正超過 30% 以

來，相信⼤多公司估值都回到合理區間，我們根據彭博社分析師預期的  1Q27-
4Q28 的季度及年度營收  YoY 增速來篩選出 20 家營收  YoY 增速明顯超過台積
電及將帶動整體科技及半導體產業營收增⻑，獲利倍增的公司，並找出受惠於
營運槓桿會三率三升（營收增⻑率，⽑利率，營業利潤率都同步年增改善）的
公司，以及營收獲利的⼤幅增⻑能持續到  2H27-2028 年但估值合理的公司。

及消耗更多測試材料，測試材料及測試設備公司如創新服務，旺矽，鴻勁，雍

三率三升：⽽均熱⽚健策，鑽頭公司尖點，銅箔基板廠台光電，台燿，聯茂，
ABF 載板廠的南亞 PCB 及景碩因產能利⽤率提升，產品價格因良率，短缺，成
本上漲及規格變⼤，變厚升級⽽調漲，這些公司將具有較⾼的營運槓桿，
2027-2028 年的獲利增⻑（⽑利率及營業利潤率雙漲），應該明顯超過營收增
⻑。但這個前提是 AI 數據算⼒中⼼的基礎建設投資週期不會提早反轉。
Source: Bloomberg, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
The growth for 2H27-2028 matters
根據過往的經驗，我們確實會看到產業於 2026 年營收沒啥增⻑，但 2027-

2028 ⼤幅增⻑（幫台積電做 FAU 的上詮  ( 請參考：從台積電  CPO/COUPE 的合
作對象找贏家及輸家），幫⾕歌 TPU 做設計統包⼯程服務的聯發科，幫聯發科
做探針卡 PCB 的雍智有機會），或者是 2026 年營收⼤幅增⻑（如存儲記憶體產
業），但 2027-2028 營收增⻑開始趨緩。所以我們以後於每季度初來更新彭博
社分析師對於覆蓋公司  2027-2028 年的營收預測，這樣才不會錯過潛在的倍
增飆股。
歸因於很多的精選 20 都是 2026 年的營收及獲利成⻑飆股且延續⾄ 2027 ，我們
認為關注 2H27-2028 季度營收的年增⻑變化⽐關注 2026-1H27 更重要，從
精選 20 家公司中，我們發現做設計服務的世芯， BMC 伺服器遠端控制晶⽚的
信驊， AI 伺服器的緯穎，及做通⽤伺服器及⾞⽤ PCB 銅箔基板的聯茂於  2H27-
2028 的季度營收增速有明顯趨緩的現象。

Document 421: Trade survives, but the battle for control intensifies_ 3 calls for global trade

Source PDF: all report pdf/Trade survives, but the battle for control intensifies_ 3 calls for global trade.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Global trade will remain resilient in 2027, but the focus will
shift from tariﬀs to ownership, technology and investment.
While not our base case, an escalation in an EU-China trade
war would rank among the most signiﬁcant risks to global
trade

Inga Fechner and Carsten Brzeski
Europe is undoubtedly facing a second 'China shock' – only this time around,
it's largely negative
How to read our three calls for 2027
Source: ING
ING's base call: Trade survives politics
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

our Cookie policy.

Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

Global goods trade continues to weather geopolitical shocks.
Over the past few years, trade has proved far more resilient
than global headlines suggest. We expect that resilience to
continue into 2027. Disruptions in global shipping, shifting
tariﬀ regimes and recurring geopolitical tensions have
repeatedly altered trade routes without derailing trade itself.
Strong demand for AI-related investment and infrastructure
should continue to support trade ﬂows, particularly across

Asia, while selective US-China decoupling is likely to beneﬁt
third countries that attract production and investment. For
2027, we expect global goods trade to remain positive,
expanding about 3% year-on-year. In short, trade survives
politics in 2027 because companies can still adapt faster
than governments can restrict them.
Our risky call: Ownership matters more than
trade
Tariﬀs were only the ﬁrst step. By 2027, the real battleground shifts
to capital, technology and ownership. As companies invest behind

tariﬀ walls, governments stop merely screening new investment
and begin requiring companies to restructure, separate sensitive
operations or divest existing strategic assets.
A foreign-owned battery plant, semiconductor facility, or data
centre once supported for bringing local investment and jobs is
now treated as a national-security risk. Companies that once
navigated geopolitical tensions through diversiﬁcation ﬁnd
themselves forced to choose sides. The question is no longer who

exports the most, but who controls the productive capacity on
which economies depend. Trade would continue, but the corporate
structures that have kept it moving would start to fracture as
governments alter trade ﬂows. This could potentially cause global
goods trade growth to stall or even contract in 2027.
Our bold call: EU-China trade war

This publication has been prepared by ING solely for information purposes

Read more
Download
Included in the following bundle
The EU and China enter an outright trade conﬂict. Europe abandons

its largely open-market approach and increasingly applies
reciprocity principles to market access and investment. Chinese
ﬁrms investing in strategic sectors face mandatory 50:50 joint
ventures, local ownership requirements and growing restrictions on
acquisitions.
At the same time, the EU imposes broad tariﬀs on Chinese imports,
with rates reaching 50% in politically sensitive sectors such as
vehicles, chemicals, machinery and electronics. China retaliates

with measures of similar scale.
The consequences are signiﬁcant. China accounts for roughly 15%
of global goods trade, while the EU accounts for around 13.5%.
Together they represent almost 30% of world goods trade. With the
EU being China's largest trading partner in goods and China being
the EU's second-largest trading partner in goods, a full-scale trade
conﬂict would rank among the most consequential economic
conﬂicts of the modern era.

3
《歐元投資關鍵-法國政治風險令歐元走弱》
10月以來歐元區一連串的動盪導致歐元走弱。除了
西班牙提前大選，法國政治局勢惡化，極右派勝出
機率上升加學生示威遊行演變成各地零星暴動，經
濟下行風險升溫。法國政府提出目標將赤字占GDP
比從5.4%降至5%的方案，但反對黨已表態不支持。
目前執政黨在下議院非多數，財政政策停擺風險導
致公債殖利率大幅上升。法國10年期公債殖利率與
德國公債殖利率利差創2011年歐債危機最嚴重時期
以來最高水準。本行認為，法國右翼已放棄脫歐，
且強調財政紀律將遵守歐盟規範，法國選後議會仍

需籌組多數聯盟，財政惡化風險可控。
總經方面，歐元區經濟基本面持續轉佳。10月PMI
顯示經濟持續擴張，出口訂單連續第3個月增加，顯
示外需逐步改善。10月PMI對應歐元區GDP季增率
約0.3%，為近3年相對穩健的季度表現。通膨方面，
能源成本持續上升，企業為維持獲利而調升售價，
高利率逐漸抑制需求。德國央行最新通膨報告顯示，
目前複製2021-22年高通膨階段的可能性不大，但
通膨風險明顯偏向上行。展望後續，ECB 10月會議
維持利率不變，但通膨的僵固性將支持12月升息，
歐洲與美利差預期小幅收斂。基本面繼續支持ECB
升息的環境下，預計美歐利差收斂將提供歐元支撐，
短線政治風險提供逢低布局時機，4Q26目標價1.19。

《英鎊投資關鍵-英國經濟動能逐漸改善》
10月經濟數據方面，英國PMI初值顯示經濟動能持

創25個月新高，製造業PMI升至52.8。新訂單成長
創2024年9月以來新高，帶動企業活動加速擴張，
並推動就業人數自2024年9月以來首度恢復成長。
然而，投入成本升至6月以來最高水準。主要受能源、
原物料與運輸成本上揚所帶動，且中東衝突與供應
鏈干擾延長交貨時間。企業對一年景氣展望樂觀，
但程度仍低於長期平均。經濟韌性增強及通膨壓力
居高不下，使英國央行將維持在貨幣緊縮的路徑，

且2027年進一步升息的可能性上升。對於英鎊的前
景逐漸樂觀，英鎊兌美元年底目標1.34。

• 4Q26美元兌瑞郎目標價0.8
5
《瑞郎投資關鍵-瑞士景氣連續第7個月擴張》
瑞士9月製造業PMI下滑1.8點至55.3，但連續第7個
月維持在擴張區間。本次下滑主因產出、供應商交貨
時間及就業分項走弱。訂單積壓指數則持穩於57.0，
顯示需求仍具韌性，但企業持續去化庫存，原材料與
成品庫存皆低於50。採購價格指數升至70，反映電
子零組件及原物料價格上漲，中東地緣政治風險及AI
熱潮亦推升成本壓力。服務業PMI由58.3升至59.8，
維持強勁成長，新訂單與商業活動改善，就業指數升
至2025年4月以來高點。採購價格及銷售價格同步明
顯上升，顯示服務業通膨壓力有增溫跡象。整體而言，

瑞士通膨及成長前景溫和，央行並未因地緣政治風險
而強調通膨壓力，利差擴大為瑞郎走弱主因。央行對
升息態度保守，外需回溫與製造業改善正帶動瑞士經
濟展望持續好轉。4Q26美元兌瑞郎維持目標價0.8。

《日圓投資關鍵-股市波動升溫帶動避險情緒》
1個月以來全球科技股明顯回檔，美元兌日圓也從
160以上回到158，匯市在美國期中選舉前表現穩定，
日圓小幅偏強但呈區間整理格局。
日本央行在10月份的會議上維持政策不變。潛在通
膨和中長期通膨預期均已達到2%。日本央行也重申
金融環境依然寬鬆的觀點，這表明與聯準會和歐洲央
行相比，日本央行將更傾向於進一步升息。
10月22日那斯達克當日重挫-3%帶動日圓一度大幅
走強，但升幅未如7月及9月政府干預匯市之規模。
短線日圓投機部位已轉為淨多單，市場繼續預期日本
政府對於穩定匯市的決心。財務大臣片山表示，美元
兌日圓匯率較先前接近164時有所改善，當局將對無

USDJPY升至160以上，日圓買入干預的風險依然很

蘇子錚 
Alex.Su@yuanta.com 
黃彥儒 
erichyj@yuanta.com

3Q26 毛利率低於預期，主因 AI 伺服器營收比重提升 
廣達 3Q26 營收為 1.15 兆元，季增 8%、年增 125%；毛利率 4.8%，季減
0.3 個百分點、年減 2.1 個百分點，儘管伺服器營收比重提升，帶動營收再
次成長，但受到零組件漲價影響，毛利率持續承壓，3Q26 稅後淨利 284 億
元，季減 1%、年增 73%，EPS 7.08 元，優於元大預期 2%。展望 4Q26，
管理層認為，AI Server 營收將持續季增，受惠於 Vera Rubin 帶動 ASP 提
升，但因進入新舊產品轉換期，出貨量將下降，通用型伺服器則維持季增，
但筆電呈季減，且毛利率短期不易明顯回升。預估廣達 4Q26 營收為 1.02
兆，季減 9%、年增 60%，EPS 5.27 元，季減 26%、年減 9%。 
AI 伺服器成最大業務，但毛利率仍有疑慮 
展望 2027 年，管理層預期 AI Server 營收呈高雙位數年增，受惠 Vera 
Rubin 於 1Q27 進入較大量出貨階段，及既有 GB300、AMD 與 ASIC 平台
持續貢獻；通用型伺服器受惠 CSP 擴大一般運算基礎建設，營收及出貨量
均預期雙位數成長。公司預估 2027 年伺服器營收占比將提升至 85–90%，
其中 AI Server 占 Server 營收超過 80%，而筆電出貨量將持續衰退。我們
認為，廣達明年仍會受惠 AI 伺服器強勁需求帶動營收成長，但受 Buy & Sell
銷售模式，將對毛利率有較大衝擊。預估 2027 年營收 5.19 兆元，年增
30%，EPS 28.78 元，年增 16%。 
營收穩健持續成長，毛利率仍看客戶意願 
明年 AI 及 ASIC 伺服器仍為廣達主要成長動能，除 Vera Rubin 量產，AMD 
MI400 系列也會陸續生產，但考慮毛利率壓力擴大，及費用提升，我們下修
廣達 2026/27 年獲利 2%/1%，後續仍觀察客戶是否願意將銷售模式改為
Consignment，維持廣達買進評等，目標價下修至 345 元，以 12 倍本益比
的 2027 年每股盈餘推得(前次為 15 x 2026 年 EPS)。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 毛利率下滑至 4.8%，主因零組件漲價影響，且 AI 伺服器營收比
重提升，產品組合轉差。 
⧫  
4Q26 因新舊產品轉換期影響，AI 伺服器出貨量下滑，但因單價提升帶
動伺服器營收成長。 
⧫  
AI、ASIC 仍為明年最大成長動能，但毛利率壓力仍需持續關注，維持
買進評等，目標價 345 元。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：ODM/EMS 代工 2026 年 11 月 12 日 Quanta 第2 頁，共 17頁  
營運分析  
新舊產品轉換期影響出貨，但單價提升帶動伺服器營收成長 
廣達 3Q26 營收為 1.15 兆元，季增 8%、年增 125%；毛利率 4.8%，季減 0.3 個百分點、年減 2.1
個百分點，3Q26 稅後淨利 284 億元，季減 1%、年增 73%，EPS 7.08 元，優於元大預期 2%。 
主要受到筆電原物料成本上升、出貨量季減 17%至 960 萬台，導致經濟規模下降，以及 AI Server
高 ASP 產品占比提升、Buy & Sell 模式稀釋毛利率影響。營業利益 322 億元，季減 6.0%、年增
75.3%，營益率 2.89%，季減 0.41 個百分點，主要反映毛利率下滑及新世代 AI Server 研發費用增
加。 
展望 4Q26，管理層預期 AI Server 營收將持續季增，主要受惠新世代產品 ASP 提升，惟受到新舊
平台轉換影響，高運算密度機櫃出貨數量預估較 3Q26 略減，較大量出貨將落在 1Q27；通用型伺
服器出貨量則預期季增。筆電出貨量預估呈雙位數季減，但降幅較 3Q26 收斂。獲利方面，隨高
ASP AI Server 產品占比持續提升，加上筆電零組件成本上升，預期 4Q26 毛利率將較 3Q26 略降，
但公司目標營益率大致維持 3Q26 水準。
